## MÔ-KʽO DJÜN FOH-ING SHÜ. <u>馬可</u>傳福音書

### Mk. I.

<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>JING-MING-GO</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>福音個</rb><rt>foh-ing-go</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>寫落的</rb><rt>sia-lôh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打發</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>前頭個</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>Kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鋪直</rb><rt>pʽu-dzih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>街道</rb><rt>ka-dao</rt></ruby>。』」 

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>回心轉意個</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腰縛裏</rb><rt>iao-bo-li</rt><rb>繫勒</rb><rt>kyi-leh</rt><rb>皮個</rb><rt>bi-go</rt><rb>繳身帶</rb><rt>kyiao-sing-ta</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>蜜糖</rb><rt>mih-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>後如</rb><rt>ʽEo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>跪帶倒</rb><rt>gyü-tæn-tao</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>鞋帶</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>水個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>走上來</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>親愛個</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>得意個</rb><rt>teh-i-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 27. 耶（穌）後（頭）27（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, hweh-kʽæ, *ʽôh*, lih-kʽæ. 或話，豁開，或，裂開。（原註）</sup> 

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>隨手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>逼</rb><rt>pih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* *Mo-kwe* *Ming-z*. 就是魔鬼名字。（原註）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>IAH-ʽEN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神國個</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>近攏來了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 30. 耶（穌）後（頭）30（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>抲魚個</rb><rt>kʽô-ng-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>Keng-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走勒</rb><rt>tseo-leh</rt><rb>過去點</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西庇太</u>個</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>補</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>網</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>隨手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳勒</rb><rt>eo-leh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>僱來個</rb><rt>kwu-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船裏</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>Tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 31. 耶（穌）後（頭）31（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的個</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>哎</rb><rt>ʽE</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>Vong</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>Ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活撞活顛</rb><rt>weh-djông-weh-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>懷</rb><rt>wæ</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講來講去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>新鮮</rb><rt>sing-sin</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名聲</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>揚開</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>四凸亂圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>安得烈</u>個</rb><rt>En-teh-lih-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西門</u>個</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>丈姆</rb><rt>dziang-m</rt><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>潮熱</rb><rt>dziao-nyih</rt><rb>睏間</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拉</rb><rt>l̆a</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>潮熱</rb><rt>dziao-nyih</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>退了</rb><rt>tʽe-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>*nyü-nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>落山</rb><rt>lôh-sæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>生病</rb><rt>sang-bing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>入魔個</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>合城裏</rb><rt>ʽeh-zing-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>號數</rb><rt>ʽao-su</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏龍送</rb><rt>u-long-song</rt><rb>早早</rb><rt>tsao-tsao</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>隨後</rb><rt>ze-ʽeo</rt><rb>跟勒去</rb><rt>keng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着仔</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>近點</rb><rt>gying-tin</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出來個</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>統</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>邊</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>跪倒</rb><rt>gyü-tao</rt><rb>邊</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出來</rb><rt>sing-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>褪掉</rb><rt>tʽeng-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>乾淨了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叮囑</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>44</sup> 「<ruby><rb>千定</rb><rt>Tsʽin-ding</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>傳來傳去</rb><rt>djün-læ-djün-kyʽi</rt><rb>揚開了</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>出管</rb><rt>cʽih-kwun</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四凸亂圈個</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mk. II.

<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百農</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>門口頭</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo-den</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>空</rb><rt>kʽong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抬勒</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>近身弗攏</rb><rt>gying-sing-feh-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>*zông-deo*</rt><rb>𢳚開</rb><rt>hyiao-kʽæ</rt><rb>屋頂</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>拆</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>鋪板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>睏的個</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吊落來</rb><rt>tiao-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>獨</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>鋪板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>？<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>赦罪個</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：）<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>鋪板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>鋪板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>走出去了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>驚駭</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走過去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞勒腓</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leh-fi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>戶糧房</rb><rt>wu-liang-vông</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>Keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起身</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>利未</u>個</rb><rt>Li-vi-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收錢糧</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>康健個</rb><rt>Kʽông-gyin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>醫生</rb><rt>i-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有病個</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要個</rb><rt>*iao-go*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>*eo*</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>*we-sing-cün-i*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>禁食個</rb><rt>kying-zih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>賀</rb><rt>ʽO</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>將來個</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>禁食其</rb><rt>kying-zih-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>補</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>舊</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>補的個</rb><rt>pu-tih-go</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>綳碎</rb><rt>pang-se</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>舊</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>*i-zông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>破綻</rb><rt>pʽo-dzæn</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>大了</rb><rt>do-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>舊</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>爆開</rb><rt>pao-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>倒掉</rb><rt>tao-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弄環</rb><rt>long-wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Sing, *ʽôh* *wô*, ngang-sao-sao, m-neh ngô-kwông-go. 新，或話，硬燥燥，嘸得砑光個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>麥田裏</rb><rt>mah-din-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>摘</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>麥佩</rb><rt>mah-be</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>喏</rb><rt>Nô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>缺少</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讀過</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>26</sup> ### Mk. III.

</sup><ruby><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拉住</rb><rt>l̆a-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大糊的了</rb><rt>do-vu-tih-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別西卜</rb><rt>Bih-si-poh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>附的個</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>鬼王</rb><rt>kwe-wông</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏來</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>一國</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>分裂開</rb><rt>feng-lih-kʽæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一國</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>立弗牢</rb><rt>lih-feh-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>分裂開</rb><rt>feng-lih-kʽæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>立弗牢</rb><rt>lih-feh-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>分裂開</rb><rt>feng-lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也是</rb><rt>yia-z</rt><rb>立弗牢</rb><rt>lih-feh-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>好佬個</rb><rt>hao-lao-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搶奪</rb><rt>tsʽiang-deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>縛住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>搶奪</rb><rt>tsʽiang-deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>*tong-si*</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>赦弗掉個</rb><rt>sô-feh-diao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>刑罰</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的個</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, joh-nying. 或話，熟人。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z*, kwe-wông. 就是鬼王。（原註）</sup>

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>坐東</rb><rt>zo-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>坐東個</rb><rt>zo-tong-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>！<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」
