<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>離掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>*nen-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*YIU-SING*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神國</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>IH</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>路街裏</rb><rt>lu-ka-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奔攏來</rb><rt>peng-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跪倒</rb><rt>gyü-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問問</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>偷盜</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>謊告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>索詐</rb><rt>soh-tsô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>』。 」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>顧着個</rb><rt>kwu-djôh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>失色</rb><rt>shih-seh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>憂悶相</rb><rt>iu-meng-siang</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>！」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>Siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>倚侍</rb><rt>i-z</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>！<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>駱駝</rb><rt>Loh-do</rt><rb>穿過</rb><rt>cʽün-ko</rt><rb>針眼</rb><rt>tsing-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>能夠個</rb><rt>*neng-keo-go*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>無所不能</rb><rt>vu-sô-peh-neng</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>棄掉仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>爹孃</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今世</rb><rt>kying-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍個</rb><rt>be-go</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>逼難個</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>下世</rb><rt>ʽô-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>*teh-djôh*</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>在前個</rb><rt>dzæ-zin-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落後</rb><rt>lôh-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>*hyü-to*</rt><rb>在後個</rb><rt>dzæ-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>ZÔNG</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>驚駭</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>解去</rb><rt>ka-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>別國人</rb><rt>*bih-koh-nying*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>饞唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>榮華個</rb><rt>yüong-wô-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>借手邊</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求個</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>能夠個</rb><rt>Neng-keo-go</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受個</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>借手邊</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*Ah-tia*</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>預備好間個</rb><rt>yü-be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>別國</rb><rt>Bih-koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>壓實</rb><rt>ah-jih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>執掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>頭個</rb><rt>deo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>所出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>替代</rb><rt>tʽi-dæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh* *wô*, tso wông-ti go. 或話，做皇帝個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴底買</rb><rt>Pô-Ti-mæ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>底買</u>個</rb><rt>Ti-mæ-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>討飯</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>可憐</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>Hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>謳勒</rb><rt>eo-leh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可憐</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立落</rb><rt>lih-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>Hah-ts</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>Hah-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拉波尼</rb><rt>Lah-po-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>53</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>*Hah-ts*</rt><rb>馬上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依路</rb><rt>i-lu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh* *wô*, i ng hao. 或話，醫爾好。（原註）</sup>
