### Mk. XI.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯法基</rb><rt>Pah-fah-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>對頭個</rb><rt>te-deo-go</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>樁間</rb><rt>công-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從來</rb><rt>dzong-læ</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>騎過</rb><rt>gyi-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>解仔</rb><rt>ka-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牽勒來</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>啥幹</rb><rt>soh-ken</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>門外</rb><rt>meng-nga</rt><rb>路口頭</rb><rt>lu-kʽeo-den</rt><rb>樁間</rb><rt>công-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>立間個</rb><rt>lih-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>應許了</rb><rt>ing-hyü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>牽勒</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>驢子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>騎上去</rb><rt>gyi-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>鋪</rb><rt>pʽu</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有星</rb><rt>wa-yiu-sing</rt><rb>摘了</rb><rt>tsôh-leh</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>鋪</rb><rt>pʽu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路裏</rb><rt>lu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>前前後後個</rb><rt>zin-zin-ʽeo-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝讚個</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>！<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝讚個</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt></ruby>！」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>看過</rb><rt>kʽen-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>肚飢了</rb><rt>du-kyi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>Yün-yün</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>葉</rb><rt>yih</rt><rb>來的個</rb><rt>læ-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>樹裏</rb><rt>jü-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>葉</rb><rt>yih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>別樣告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>買賣</rb><rt>ma-ma</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>推倒</rb><rt>tʽe-tao</rt><rb>兌</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>櫃頭</rb><rt>gyü-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>椅子</rb><rt>ü-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>萬國人</rb><rt>væn-koh-nying</rt><rb>禱告個</rb><rt>tao-kao-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>？』 <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>賊窠</rb><rt>zeh-kʽo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-wô* væn-koh-nying iao tsʽing-hwu gyi z tao-kao-go oh-lô. 或話，萬國人要稱呼其是禱告個屋落。（原註）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>根頭</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>枯了</rb><rt>kwʽu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸頭到</rb><rt>sing-deo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喏</rb><rt>nô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>咒罵個</rb><rt>tsiu-mô-go</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>枯了</rb><rt>kwʽu-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>移過去</rb><rt>yi-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>投落</rb><rt>deo-lôh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>疑惑</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>成功</rb><rt>dzing-kong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>*z-jün*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>成功</rb><rt>dzing-kong</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禱告</rb><rt>Tao-kao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求個</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨氣</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>過失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>過失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\]*Peng-veng* *wô*, Kæ-yiu Jing-ming-go sing-teh. 本文話，該有神明個信德。（原註）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>：<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>走來走去個</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？』<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mk. XII.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>種</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>一園</rb><rt>ih-yün</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>打勒</rb><rt>tang-leh</rt><rb>槍笆</rb><rt>tsʽiang-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>酒榨窞</rb><rt>tsiu-tsô-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>高樓</rb><rt>kao-leo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>租出</rb><rt>tsu-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>*siu*</rt><rb>租</rb><rt>*tsu*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>園裏個</rb><rt>yün-li-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞傷</rb><rt>kʽang-sông</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>倒了霉</rb><rt>tao-leh-me</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>*nu-boh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>親愛個</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>殺勒</rb><rt>sah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>園外</rb><rt>yün-nga</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>居園個</rb><rt>ke-yün-go</rt><rb>業主</rb><rt>nyih-cü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>辦法</rb><rt>bæn-fah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>種田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>葡萄園</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>10, 11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇節</rb><rt>Keh-tsih</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>起屋個</rb><rt>Kyʽi-oh-go</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>*oh*</rt><rb>角落頭</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt><rb>頭一塊</rb><rt>deo-ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>稀奇個</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-go</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讀過</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>*Keh-pæn*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇黨</rb><rt>keh-tông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>箍套圈</rb><rt>kwʽu-tʽao-kyʽün</rt><rb>套</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懼脅</rb><rt>gyü-hyih</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>人家個</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>實實惠惠</rb><rt>jih-jih-we-we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>投</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>錢糧</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>投</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>投</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>？」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>假好</rb><rt>kô-hao</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>番餅</rb><rt>fæn-ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>頭子</rb><rt>deo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國號</rb><rt>koh-ʽao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰儂個</rb><rt>jü-noh-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] Kæ-sah, *ziu-z* *Lo-mô* *wông-ti-go* *koh-ʽao*. <u>該撒</u>，就是<u>羅馬</u>皇帝個國號。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Keh-kwʽe* *fæn-ping* *dzih* *ih-din* *nying-ts*. <sup>2</sup> 該塊番餅值一鈿銀子。（原註）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>剩勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>交落東</rb><rt>gao-lôh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>傳宗接代</rb><rt>djün-cong-tsih-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>*ih-veng*</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>抬勒</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>交落</rb><rt>gao-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>第二個</rb><rt>Di-nyi-go</rt><rb>抬勒</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第三個</rb><rt>di-sæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>和七個</rb><rt>ʽO-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>交落</rb><rt>gao-lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>錯了</rb><rt>tsʽo-de</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>娶進</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>Tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>箇篇</rb><rt>keh-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讀過</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>死人個</rb><rt>si-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活人個</rb><rt>weh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>錯了</rb><rt>tsʽo-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答勒</rb><rt>we-teh-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一條</rb><rt>ih-diao</rt></ruby>？」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>獨一位</rb><rt>doh-ih-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>精神</rb><rt>tsing-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>才情</rb><rt>dzæ-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頭一個</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>相像個</rb><rt>siang-ziang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩樣</rb><rt>liang-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好個</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>精神</rb><rt>tsing-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愛敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>鄰舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>福禮</rb><rt>foh-li</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>回答勒</rb><rt>we-teh-leh</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>離</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遠</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>教訓個</rb><rt>kao-hyüing-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>踏腳凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>教訓個</rb><rt>Kao-hyüing-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>謹防</rb><rt>kying-bông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>長長</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>，<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>上位</rb><rt>zông-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒筵上</rb><rt>tsiu-yin-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>頭位</rb><rt>deo-we</rt></ruby>，<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>吞喫</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿個</rb><rt>lao-nyüing-go</rt><rb>家計</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假戲頭</rb><rt>kô-hyi-deo</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>漫長</rb><rt>mæn-dziang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受罪</rb><rt>ziu-ze</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>錢庫</rb><rt>dzin-kwʽu</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>錢庫裏</rb><rt>dzin-kwʽu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>Hyü-to</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>丟勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>窮</rb><rt>gyüong</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>值</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>一厘</rb><rt>ih-li</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>錢庫裏</rb><rt>dzin-kwʽu-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>窮窮</rb><rt>gyüong-gyüong</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>丟勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>多頭</rb><rt>to-deo</rt><rb>丟點的</rb><rt>tiu-tin-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缺利利</rb><rt>kyʽüih-li-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>丟進的了</rb><rt>tiu-tsing-tih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>和家計</rb><rt>ʽo-kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mk. XIII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>！」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>Tsiang-læ</rt><rb>連只</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>幾時</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>話話</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>應驗</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>冒勒</rb><rt>mao-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>打仗個</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>搖惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>末個</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未了</rb><rt>mi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>反亂</rb><rt>fæn-lön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>災難個</rb><rt>tsæ-næn-go</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>公會裏</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>皇帝個</rb><rt>wông-ti-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>傳開</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>萬國個</rb><rt>væn-koh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>*yü-sin*</rt><rb>愁</rb><rt>zeo</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>*kông-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>*pô*</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>*song*</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>*bæn*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>*si-ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>刻忖</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>底</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但以理</rb><rt>Dæn-yi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>可惡個</rb><rt>kʽo-u-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山裏</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋頂</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田頭</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大肚</rb><rt>do-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>喫奶個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-na-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>冬天</rb><rt>tong-tʽin</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍難</rb><rt>ʽo-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>開天闢地</rb><rt>kʽæ-tʽin-pʽih-di</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日腳</rb><rt>nyih-kyiah</rt><rb>短點</rb><rt>tön-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>救弗來</rb><rt>kyiu-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日腳</rb><rt>nyih-kyiah</rt><rb>短個</rb><rt>tön-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>*ta-diao*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>當</rb><rt>Tông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>災難個</rb><rt>tsæ-næn-go</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>發光</rb><rt>fah-kwông</rt></ruby>，<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跌落來</rb><rt>tih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天個</rb><rt>tʽin-go</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>四方</rb><rt>s-fông</rt><rb>挑選個</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>嫩起來</rb><rt>neng-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葉爿</rb><rt>yih-bæn</rt><rb>爆出來</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>夏天</rb><rt>ʽô-tʽin</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>是介個</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>動作起來</rb><rt>dong-tsôh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>*koh*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh-tsia*, *Nying-go* *Ng-ts*. 或者人個兒子。（原註）</sup>

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天上個</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh-go*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>*Nying-go*</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*Ng-ts*</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>*kao-dæ*</rt><rb>各人個</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>管門個</rb><rt>kwun-meng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>夜到頭</rb><rt>yia-tao-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>半夜過</rb><rt>pun-yia-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>雞</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>啼個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>睏熟東</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>。」

### Mk. XIV.

<ruby><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>YÜ-YÜIH-TSIH</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>無酵節</rb><rt>vu-kao-tsih</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>計較</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>鬧事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>坐席個</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>貴</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>𣥼的個</rb><rt>tsi-tih-go</rt><rb>玉個</rb><rt>nyüoh-go</rt><rb>盒子</rb><rt>ʽeh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敲開了</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽæ-leh</rt><rb>盒子</rb><rt>ʽeh-ts</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>頭裏</rb><rt>deo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>費掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>三十多</rb><rt>sæn-jih-to</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>Tsʽia</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>擔</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>心事</rb><rt>sing-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>窮人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>喪事</rb><rt>sông-z</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽEn-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>傳開個</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>Jih-nyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加略</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>機會</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>無酵節個</rb><rt>VU-KAO-TSIH-GO</rt><rb>頭日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一瓶</rb><rt>ih-bing</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>挈的</rb><rt>kyʽih-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走進個</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇份</rb><rt>keh-veng</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>箇間</rb><rt>keh-kæn</rt><rb>客房</rb><rt>kʽah-vông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？』<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>擺設好了</rb><rt>pa-shih-hao-liao</rt><rb>一間</rb><rt>ih-kæn</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>樓頂</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>備好間</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>備好間</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn</rt></ruby>。
