<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>個打個</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>盆子裏</rb><rt>beng-ts-li</rt><rb>下手個</rb><rt>ʽô-siu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>原是</rb><rt>nyün-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>便宜</rb><rt>bin-i</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>饅頭</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福仔</rb><rt>coh-foh-ts</rt><rb>𤖼開</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝謝仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喝了</rb><rt>hah-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>新約個</rb><rt>sing-iah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>*hyüih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>流出來個</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神國</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>聖詩</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>唱好</rb><rt>tsʽông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>厭憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聖書裏</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>看羊個</rb><rt>Kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>厭憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>當夜</rb><rt>tông-yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>啼</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>兩伐</rb><rt>liang-vah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>煞扣</rb><rt>sah-kʽeo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>卻勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>客西馬尼</rb><rt>Kʽeh-si-mô-nyi</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>等東</rb><rt>teng-tong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>*sing-li*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>懍懍動</rb><rt>ling-ling-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>極頭</rb><rt>gyih-deo</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>進起</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好使得個</rb><rt>hao-s-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>亞爸</rb><rt>Üô-pô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>能夠個</rb><rt>neng-keo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>*gyiu*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>總是</rb><rt>tsong-z</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>Tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>睏熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>睏熟了</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-de</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靈性</rb><rt>Ling-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>情願個</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>軟弱</rb><rt>nyün-ziah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話法</rb><rt>wô-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>轉來</rb><rt>Cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>睏熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>眯攏去了</rb><rt>mi-long-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>Di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>*ze*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將息</rb><rt>tsiang-sih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>夠了</rb><rt>keo-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>罪人個</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>近攏來了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>當時</rb><rt>Tông-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>長老個</rb><rt>tsiang-lao-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>Ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉個</rb><rt>diao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>暗號</rb><rt>en-ʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗅嘴個</rb><rt>hyüong-cü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牢牢靠靠</rb><rt>lao-lao-kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>帶勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>Bông-pin</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拔出</rb><rt>bah-cʽih</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斬</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>強盜</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>*tso-fah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>聖書裏個</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逃走了</rb><rt>dao-tseo-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>細</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>圍勒</rb><rt>yü-leh</rt><rb>出膊個</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>*kyi-sing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>營兵</rb><rt>ying-ping</rt><rb>揸</rb><rt>cô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>褪掉了</rb><rt>tʽeng-diao-leh</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出膊個</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt><rb>逃走了</rb><rt>dao-tseo-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>衙門裏</rb><rt>*ngô-meng-li*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>遠</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>遠</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>衙門裏</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烘烘火</rb><rt>hong-hong-ho</rt></ruby>。

<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>對</rb><rt>te</rt><rb>證</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>弗符</rb><rt>feh-vu</rt></ruby>。<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>58</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>*sing*-din</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>毀掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗符</rb><rt>feh-vu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>當中央</rb><rt>tông-cong-nyiang</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>61</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>62</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>乘勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>別人個</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>？<sup>64</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>饞唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拳頭</rb><rt>gyün-deo</rt><rb>敲</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>摑</rb><rt>kwah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>66</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>下頭</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明堂裏</rb><rt>ming-dông-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>67</sup> <ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>烘火</rb><rt>hong-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同隊個</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>68</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>前明堂</rb><rt>zin-ming-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>啼了</rb><rt>di-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>69</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做隊個</rb><rt>tso-de-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>70</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做隊</rb><rt>tso-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>對個</rb><rt>te-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>71</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>甚大咒</rb><rt>zing-do-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>72</sup> <ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>第二遍</rb><rt>di-nyi-pin</rt><rb>啼了</rb><rt>di-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>記得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講過個</rb><rt>kông-ko-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>雞</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>啼</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>兩伐</rb><rt>liang-vah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>哭了</rb><rt>kʽoh-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mk. XV.

<ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>TSAO</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>商量好</rb><rt>siang-liang-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>縛勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>帶出去</rb><rt>ta-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>節肯個</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老老</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>餘黨</rb><rt>yü-tông</rt><rb>造反個</rb><rt>dzao-fæn-go</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>縛間</rb><rt>bo-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>造反個</rb><rt>dzao-fæn-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>*Pe-lah-to*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>老例</rb><rt>lao-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗過</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt><rb>解上來個</rb><rt>ka-zông-læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>攛掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>惡</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>謳勒</rb><rt>eo-leh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>釘</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>安得</rb><rt>en-teh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>明堂後</rb><rt>ming-dông-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>官廳</rb><rt>kwun-tʽing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>合營個</rb><rt>ʽeh-ying-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>紅袍</rb><rt>ʽong-*bao*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>編出</rb><rt>pin-cʽih</rt><rb>一頂</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恭喜</rb><rt>kong-hyi</rt></ruby>！」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>蘆竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>饞唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>跪倒</rb><rt>gyü-tao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>取笑好仔</rb><rt>Cʽü-siao-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>剝掉</rb><rt>poh-diao</rt><rb>紅袍</rb><rt>*ʽong-bao*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖出去</rb><rt>tʽo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh wô*, wông-kong. 或話，皇宮。（原註）</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞力山大</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>魯孚</u>個</rb><rt>Lu-fu-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>田畈裏</rb><rt>din-pæn-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>背</rb><rt>pe</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>各各他</rb><rt>Kôh-kôh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>骷顱頭</rb><rt>kwʽu-lu-deo</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>Do-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>沒藥</rb><rt>meh-yiah</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>釘上</rb><rt>Ting-zông</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>九</rb><rt>Kyiu</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>貼</rb><rt>tʽiah</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>條子</rb><rt>diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>NYING-GO</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>WÔNG-TI</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>強盜</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>借手邊</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇節</rb><rt>keh-tsih</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>算進</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>」 <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效驗</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>走過個</rb><rt>Tseo-ko-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>搖搖</rb><rt>yiao-yiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>哎</rb><rt>ʽE</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>毀掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起好</rb><rt>kyʽi-hao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走落來</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>講來講去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>嘲笑</rb><rt>tsao-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>救過了</rb><rt>kyiu-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看見仔</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>釘間個</rb><rt>ting-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>𧮙罵</rb><rt>zoh-mô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>*en*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>Sæn</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>拉馬</rb><rt>lah-mô</rt><rb>撒白大尼</rb><rt>sah-bah-da-nyi</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？」 <sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立間個</rb><rt>lih-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奔起</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>海綿花</rb><rt>hæ-min-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醋</rb><rt>tsʽu</rt><rb>滲透仔</rb><rt>seng-tʽeo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戳</rb><rt>cʽoh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蘆竿裏</rb><rt>lu-ken-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>餘多</rb><rt>*yü-to*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>甮了</rb><rt>Vong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亞</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斷了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>聖殿裏個</rb><rt>*Sing*-din-li-go</rt><rb>幔帳</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>上下</rb><rt>zông-ʽô</rt><rb>𠡒裂開</rb><rt>te-lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>對面</rb><rt>te-min</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一聲</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斷了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>看間</rb><rt>kʽen-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>個</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約西</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-si-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒羅米</rb><rt>Sah-lo-mi</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>41</sup> <sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>YI-KYING</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>預備日</rb><rt>yü-be-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt></ruby>，<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞利馬太</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-li-mô-tʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>議事部裏</rb><rt>nyi-z-bu-li</rt><rb>貴重個</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神國</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>討</rb><rt>tʽao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>得知了</rb><rt>teh-cü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>。

### Mk. XVI.

<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>EN-SIH-NYIH</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>個</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>*ah-nyiang*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒羅米</rb><rt>Sah-lo-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>買了</rb><rt>ma-leh</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>*s-siu*</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>七日個</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>頭日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>頭</rb><rt>Deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>翻過了</rb><rt>fæn-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇塊</rb><rt>*keh-kwʽe*</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>*zah-deo*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>Tseo-tsing</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>長衫</rb><rt>dziang-sæn</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>Vong</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活轉來了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介個</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>墳頭</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>奔勒去</rb><rt>peng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嚇勒</rb><rt>hah-leh</rt><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>怕個</rb><rt>pʽô-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>七日個</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>頭日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>個</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同隊個</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt><rb>哭個</rb><rt>kʽoh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>鄉下</rb><rt>hyiang-ʽô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走路個</rb><rt>tseo-lu-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>各樣</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十一個</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>坐席個</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>責罰</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>相信個</rb><rt>Siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>各處</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>；<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>毒食</rb><rt>doh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>生病</rb><rt>sang-bing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講好仔</rb><rt>kông-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各到處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
